ParlaMint-BG_2018-11-27.u46	Dear colleagues, at the beginning of what Mrs Stoyanova read, there are one of the main points of controversy in the Bulgarian healthcare system. This concerns limits in the performance of medical activities, respectively under art. 3 for the performers of outpatient care and medical diagnostic laboratories and the last four for the hospital. Here we must clearly and firmly state to the Bulgarian citizens whether we accept the limitation of the activity when they need medical care and go to the performers in the amount of extra-hospital assistance. That is to say, this concerns art. 3.2.1, which allocates the established number and values to the activities of the performer of outpatient primary and specialized medical care and art. 3.2.2, which concerns already purchasing from contractors of specialized outpatient medical care volumes within the values of the previous article. I don't think there's a normal person who agrees to have a limit on what a doctor does when a needy patient goes to him and needs treatment, needs diagnostics. Art. 7e. (1) The staff of the Executive Agency shall be obliged not to disclose the circumstances and facts which have become known to them during or on the occasion of the performance of their duties, except at the written request of a public authority, where provided for in a law. (2) In relation to their obligations under para. 1 persons shall sign a declaration in a form approved by the Executive Director of the Agency. Art. 7f. (1) The activities of the Executive Agency shall be financed by budgetary resources and revenue from its own activities. (2) The budgetary resources shall be provided by a subsidy from the state budget through the budget of the Ministry of Health. (3) The Executive Agency shall be the administrator of the revenue from its own activity, which shall be formed by: 1. fines and property penalties imposed by criminal decrees entered into force, issued by the Executive Director of the Executive Agency, Medical supervision, received on its account; 2. other sources. Art. 7g. Financial resources under art. 7f, para. 3 shall be spent on the activities of the Executive Agency. 4. (1) Medical establishments may establish and participate in self-assessment systems and rating systems to assess the quality of their medical and organisational activities and financial and administrative management activities.
